# Editor control bindings

Each entry identifies the observed or explicitly supplemental control, its interaction record and any target-specific clarification. Labels grouped in one paragraph share that precise record. Shared chrome is listed where it appears; it never creates a second artifact owner. Source ordinal numbers help locate the inspected static occurrence, and are not production IDs.

<a id="h.43tcu3k036bp"></a>

Designed element outside the button/input extraction. Select/drag or open operations for the captured BlockID; use the insertion indicator and reject structural cycles.

Review the theme system checkbox; Polish the everyday controls checkbox; Check keyboard navigation checkbox → [IX-TEXT-006](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.8psqfdw3e3qx). Designed element outside the button/input extraction. Toggle only the stored checklist item. Clicking its label edits/selects text; it creates no Planner task or Study proficiency evidence.

Stage/Description/Status table; Agreed status cell; In progress status cell; Next status cell → [IX-BOARD-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.2cth9ju2xu9c). Designed element outside the button/input extraction. Address the actual cell/embedded-table object. Status text is authored table content, not a global task-state command.

flow block border; column divider; free-placement resize handles → [IX-BOARD-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.8ydf1dv7ooqb). Designed element outside the button/input extraction. These handle classes address layout only when visible for the selected block. Locked layout rejects geometry while normal flow editing stays available.

Stage/Description/Status table; Agreed status cell; In progress status cell; Next status cell → [IX-BOARD-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.2cth9ju2xu9c). Designed element outside the button/input extraction. Address the actual cell/embedded-table object. Status text is authored table content, not a global task-state command.

flow block border; column divider; free-placement resize handles → [IX-BOARD-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.8ydf1dv7ooqb). Designed element outside the button/input extraction. These handle classes address layout only when visible for the selected block. Locked layout rejects geometry while normal flow editing stays available.

Spacing before · 0 px; Spacing after · 8 px; Line · 1.5 × → [IX-TEXT-005](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.q3bcd39sbl1i). Designed element outside the button/input extraction. Unit-aware paragraph spacing fields apply to the captured selection; invalid/mixed states do not become zero.
